````ecommerce-backend/src/main/container.ts
/**
 * Everything the application needs, expressed as ports. The root supplies the
 * concrete adapters; tests supply fakes through the same shape.
 */
export type ContainerDependencies = {
  customers: CustomerRepository;
  accounts: AccountRepository;
  products: ProductRepository;
  inventory: InventoryRepository;
  carts: CartRepository;
  orders: OrderRepository;
  outbox: OutboxRepository;
  cache: Cache;
  clock: Clock;
  ids: IdGenerator;
  events: EventPublisher;
  gateway: PaymentGateway;
  idempotency: IdempotencyStore;
  unitOfWork: UnitOfWork;
};

export type UseCases = {
  register: Register;
  login: Login;
  getCustomer: GetCustomer;
  createProduct: CreateProduct;
  getProduct: GetProduct;
  listProducts: ListProducts;
  addItem: AddItem;
  removeItem: RemoveItem;
  getCart: GetCart;
  createOrder: CreateOrder;
  getOrder: GetOrder;
  cancelOrder: CancelOrder;
  processPayment: ProcessPayment;
  checkout: Checkout;
  dispatchOutbox: DispatchOutbox;
};

/**
 * The default adapter set: in-process, disposable, no external services.
 *
 * One store is shared by every repository and by the transaction adapter, so a
 * rollback can restore exactly the state the transaction started from.
 */
export function buildInMemoryDependencies(): ContainerDependencies {
  const store = createInMemoryStore();

  return {
    customers: createInMemoryCustomerRepository(store),
    accounts: createInMemoryAccountRepository(store),
    products: createInMemoryProductRepository(store),
    inventory: createInMemoryInventoryRepository(store),
    carts: createInMemoryCartRepository(store),
    orders: createInMemoryOrderRepository(store),
    outbox: createInMemoryOutboxRepository(store),
    cache: createInMemoryCache(),
    clock: createSystemClock(),
    ids: createUuidGenerator(),
    events: createConsoleEventPublisher(),
    gateway: createInMemoryPaymentGateway(),
    idempotency: createInMemoryIdempotencyStore(),
    unitOfWork: createInMemoryUnitOfWork(store),
  };
}

export type AuthDependencies = {
  hasher: PasswordHasher;
  tokens: TokenService;
};

export function buildUseCases(
  dependencies: ContainerDependencies,
  auth: AuthDependencies,
  options: { bootstrapAdminEmails?: readonly string[] } = {},
): UseCases {
  // Every use case that writes more than one row receives the same boundary.
  const transactional = {
    ids: dependencies.ids,
    clock: dependencies.clock,
    outbox: dependencies.outbox,
    unitOfWork: dependencies.unitOfWork,
  };

  const createOrder = createOrderUseCase({
    carts: dependencies.carts,
    orders: dependencies.orders,
    inventory: dependencies.inventory,
    ...transactional,
  });

  const cancelOrder = cancelOrderUseCase({
    orders: dependencies.orders,
    inventory: dependencies.inventory,
    ...transactional,
  });

  const processPayment = processPaymentUseCase({
    orders: dependencies.orders,
    inventory: dependencies.inventory,
    gateway: dependencies.gateway,
    ...transactional,
  });

  return {
    register: registerUseCase({
      customers: dependencies.customers,
      accounts: dependencies.accounts,
      hasher: auth.hasher,
      tokens: auth.tokens,
      bootstrapAdminEmails: options.bootstrapAdminEmails ?? [],
      ...transactional,
    }),
    login: loginUseCase({
      customers: dependencies.customers,
      accounts: dependencies.accounts,
      hasher: auth.hasher,
      tokens: auth.tokens,
    }),
    getCustomer: getCustomerUseCase({ customers: dependencies.customers }),
    createProduct: createProductUseCase({
      products: dependencies.products,
      inventory: dependencies.inventory,
      ids: dependencies.ids,
      clock: dependencies.clock,
      unitOfWork: dependencies.unitOfWork,
    }),
    getProduct: getProductUseCase({
      products: dependencies.products,
      cache: dependencies.cache,
    }),
    listProducts: listProductsUseCase({ products: dependencies.products }),
    addItem: addItemUseCase({
      carts: dependencies.carts,
      products: dependencies.products,
      inventory: dependencies.inventory,
      ids: dependencies.ids,
      clock: dependencies.clock,
    }),
    removeItem: removeItemUseCase({
      carts: dependencies.carts,
      clock: dependencies.clock,
    }),
    getCart: getCartUseCase({ carts: dependencies.carts }),
    createOrder,
    getOrder: getOrderUseCase({ orders: dependencies.orders }),
    cancelOrder,
    processPayment,
    checkout: checkoutUseCase({
      createOrder,
      processPayment,
      cancelOrder,
      idempotency: dependencies.idempotency,
      clock: dependencies.clock,
    }),
    dispatchOutbox: dispatchOutboxUseCase({
      outbox: dependencies.outbox,
      publisher: dependencies.events,
      clock: dependencies.clock,
    }),
  };
}

export function buildControllers(useCases: UseCases): HttpControllers {
  return {
    auth: createAuthController({
      register: useCases.register,
      login: useCases.login,
      getCustomer: useCases.getCustomer,
    }),
    customers: createCustomerController({
      getCustomer: useCases.getCustomer,
    }),
    products: createProductController({
      createProduct: useCases.createProduct,
      getProduct: useCases.getProduct,
      listProducts: useCases.listProducts,
    }),
    carts: createCartController({
      addItem: useCases.addItem,
      removeItem: useCases.removeItem,
      getCart: useCases.getCart,
    }),
    orders: createOrderController({
      createOrder: useCases.createOrder,
      getOrder: useCases.getOrder,
      cancelOrder: useCases.cancelOrder,
      processPayment: useCases.processPayment,
    }),
    checkout: createCheckoutController({ checkout: useCases.checkout }),
  };
}

export type ContainerOptions = {
  /** `null` keeps the in-memory adapters, which is the default for tests. */
  postgres: { url: string } | null;
  /** `null` keeps the in-memory cache, idempotency, and rate limiter. */
  redis: { url: string } | null;
  /** `null` publishes to stdout and runs no consumer. */
  kafka: {
    brokers: string[];
    clientId: string;
    consumerGroup: string;
  } | null;
  rateLimit: { max: number; windowSeconds: number };
  auth: {
    secret: string;
    issuer: string;
    audience: string;
    ttlSeconds: number;
    /** Register/login get a tighter budget than the rest of the API. */
    rateLimit: { max: number; windowSeconds: number };
    /** Addresses that become administrators when they register. */
    bootstrapAdminEmails: readonly string[];
  };
};

export type Container = {
  useCases: UseCases;
  controllers: HttpControllers;
  /** One verifier for the server; routes and tokens cannot disagree. */
  tokens: TokenService;
  metrics: Metrics;
  rateLimiter: RateLimiter;
  authRateLimiter: RateLimiter;
  healthChecks: HealthCheck[];
  /** Connects background consumers. Safe to call once. */
  start(): Promise<void>;
  /** Releases connections in reverse order of acquisition. */
  close(): Promise<void>;
};

/**
 * The single place that decides which adapter each port receives, and the only
 * module that knows every world at once: the application graph, persistence,
 * cache, broker, and metrics.
 *
 * Selection is configuration, not code branching inside the application:
 *
 *   DATABASE_URL → Prisma repositories + serializable unit of work
 *   REDIS_URL    → Redis cache, atomic idempotency, shared rate limiting
 *   KAFKA_BROKERS→ Kafka producer behind the outbox + an idempotent consumer
 */
export async function buildContainer({
  postgres,
  redis,
  kafka,
  rateLimit,
  auth,
}: ContainerOptions): Promise<Container> {
  const hasher = createScryptPasswordHasher();
  const tokens = createJwtTokenService({
    secret: auth.secret,
    issuer: auth.issuer,
    audience: auth.audience,
    ttlSeconds: auth.ttlSeconds,
  });
  const metrics = createMetrics();
  const healthChecks: HealthCheck[] = [];
  const shutdown: (() => Promise<void>)[] = [];

  let persistence: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof buildPrismaPersistence>> | null =
    null;
  let inbox: InboxStore = createInMemoryInboxStore();
  let cache: Cache = createInMemoryCache();
  let idempotency: IdempotencyStore = createInMemoryIdempotencyStore();
  let rateLimiter: RateLimiter = createInMemoryRateLimiter({
    limit: rateLimit.max,
    windowSeconds: rateLimit.windowSeconds,
  });
  let authRateLimiter: RateLimiter = createInMemoryRateLimiter({
    limit: auth.rateLimit.max,
    windowSeconds: auth.rateLimit.windowSeconds,
  });
  let events: EventPublisher = createConsoleEventPublisher();
  let kafkaPublisher: KafkaEventPublisher | null = null;
  let consumer: OrderEventsConsumer | null = null;

  if (postgres !== null) {
    persistence = await buildPrismaPersistence(postgres);
    const connection = persistence;
    inbox = connection.inbox;

    healthChecks.push({ name: "postgres", check: () => connection.check() });
    shutdown.push(() => connection.close());
  }

  if (redis !== null) {
    const connection = await connectRedis(redis.url);

    cache = createRedisCache(connection.client);
    idempotency = createRedisIdempotencyStore(connection.client);
    rateLimiter = createRedisRateLimiter(connection.client, {
      limit: rateLimit.max,
      windowSeconds: rateLimit.windowSeconds,
    });
    authRateLimiter = createRedisRateLimiter(connection.client, {
      limit: auth.rateLimit.max,
      windowSeconds: auth.rateLimit.windowSeconds,
    });

    healthChecks.push({ name: "redis", check: () => connection.check() });
    shutdown.push(() => connection.close());
  }

  if (kafka !== null) {
    kafkaPublisher = createKafkaEventPublisher({
      brokers: kafka.brokers,
      clientId: kafka.clientId,
    });
    events = kafkaPublisher;

    consumer = createOrderEventsConsumer({
      brokers: kafka.brokers,
      clientId: kafka.clientId,
      groupId: kafka.consumerGroup,
      inbox,
    });

    healthChecks.push({
      name: "kafka",
      check: async () => {
        await kafkaPublisher?.connect();
      },
    });

    shutdown.push(async () => {
      try {
        await consumer?.stop();
      } catch {
        // The consumer may never have connected; disconnecting is best effort.
      }
      await kafkaPublisher?.disconnect();
    });
  }

  const ports: ContainerDependencies =
    persistence === null
      ? { ...buildInMemoryDependencies(), cache, events, idempotency }
      : {
          ...persistence,
          cache,
          clock: createSystemClock(),
          ids: createUuidGenerator(),
          events,
          gateway: createInMemoryPaymentGateway(),
          idempotency,
        };

  const wired = buildUseCases(
    ports,
    { hasher, tokens },
    { bootstrapAdminEmails: auth.bootstrapAdminEmails },
  );

  // Metrics wrap outcomes, not layers: the use cases stay unaware of them.
  const useCases: UseCases = {
    ...wired,
    checkout: async (input) => {
      const result = await wired.checkout(input);

      if (result.ok) {
        metrics.recordCheckout(
          result.value.replayed ? "replayed" : "completed",
        );
      } else {
        const outcome =
          result.error.type === "payment_declined"
            ? "declined"
            : result.error.type === "checkout_in_progress"
              ? "in_progress"
              : "failed";

        metrics.recordCheckout(outcome);
      }

      return result;
    },
    dispatchOutbox: async () => {
      const result = await wired.dispatchOutbox();

      if (result.ok) {
        metrics.recordOutboxDelivery(
          result.value.published,
          result.value.failed,
          result.value.deadLettered,
        );
      }

      return result;
    },
  };

  return {
    useCases,
    controllers: buildControllers(useCases),
    tokens,
    metrics,
    rateLimiter,
    authRateLimiter,
    healthChecks,

    async start() {
      if (kafka !== null) {
        try {
          await ensureKafkaTopics({
            brokers: kafka.brokers,
            clientId: kafka.clientId,
          });
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(
            "Kafka topic bootstrap failed; relying on auto-create",
            error,
          );
        }
      }

      if (consumer === null) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        await consumer.start();
      } catch (error) {
        // The outbox keeps retrying; a consumer that cannot start should not
        // take the HTTP API down with it.
        console.error(
          "Kafka consumer failed to start; outbox delivery will retry",
          error,
        );
      }
    },

    async close() {
      for (const release of [...shutdown].reverse()) {
        try {
          await release();
        } catch (error) {
          console.error("Shutdown step failed", error);
        }
      }
    },
  };
}

````
````ecommerce-backend/src/main/grant-admin.ts
import { assignRole } from "../domain/auth/account.js";
import { loadConfig } from "../infrastructure/config/config.js";
import { buildPrismaPersistence } from "../infrastructure/persistence/prisma/prisma-dependencies.js";

/**
 * Roles are never self-assigned, and there is no public endpoint that can
 * create one. Elevation is an auditable operator action:
 *
 *   npm run admin:grant -- ada@example.com
 *   # in a deployed image:
 *   node dist/main/grant-admin.js ada@example.com
 */
async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const email = process.argv[2]?.trim().toLowerCase();

  if (email === undefined || email.length === 0) {
    console.error("Usage: npm run admin:grant -- <email>");
    process.exitCode = 1;
    return;
  }

  const config = loadConfig();

  if (!config.ok) {
    console.error(config.error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
    return;
  }

  if (config.value.databaseUrl === null) {
    console.error("DATABASE_URL is required to change a role");
    process.exitCode = 1;
    return;
  }

  const persistence = await buildPrismaPersistence({
    url: config.value.databaseUrl,
  });

  try {
    const customer = await persistence.customers.findByEmail(email);

    if (customer === null) {
      console.error(`No customer is registered with ${email}`);
      process.exitCode = 1;
      return;
    }

    const account = await persistence.accounts.findByCustomerId(customer.id);

    if (account === null) {
      console.error(`${email} has no credential record`);
      process.exitCode = 1;
      return;
    }

    const updated = assignRole(account, "admin", new Date().toISOString());

    if (!updated.ok) {
      console.error(updated.error.message);
      process.exitCode = 1;
      return;
    }

    await persistence.accounts.save(updated.value);
    console.log(`Granted admin to ${email} (${customer.id})`);
    console.log(
      "Existing access tokens keep their old role until they expire.",
    );
  } finally {
    await persistence.close();
  }
}

await main();

````
````ecommerce-backend/src/main/server.ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { loadConfig } from "../infrastructure/config/config.js";
import { buildHttpApp } from "../infrastructure/http/fastify.js";
import {
  createOutboxWorker,
  type OutboxWorker,
} from "../infrastructure/outbox/outbox-worker.js";
import { buildContainer } from "./container.js";

/** One shutdown path for both termination signals. */
function registerShutdown(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  worker: OutboxWorker,
  closeContainer: () => Promise<void>,
): void {
  const shutdown = async (signal: NodeJS.Signals): Promise<void> => {
    app.log.info({ signal }, "Shutting down");
    await worker.stop();
    await app.close();
    await closeContainer();
    process.exit(0);
  };

  for (const signal of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"] as const) {
    process.once(signal, () => {
      void shutdown(signal);
    });
  }
}

async function start(): Promise<void> {
  const config = loadConfig();

  if (!config.ok) {
    console.error(config.error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
    return;
  }

  let container;

  try {
    container = await buildContainer({
      postgres:
        config.value.databaseUrl === null
          ? null
          : { url: config.value.databaseUrl },
      redis:
        config.value.redisUrl === null ? null : { url: config.value.redisUrl },
      kafka:
        config.value.kafkaBrokers === null
          ? null
          : {
              brokers: config.value.kafkaBrokers,
              clientId: config.value.kafkaClientId,
              consumerGroup: config.value.kafkaConsumerGroup,
            },
      rateLimit: config.value.rateLimit,
      auth: {
        secret: config.value.auth.jwtSecret,
        issuer: config.value.auth.jwtIssuer,
        audience: config.value.auth.jwtAudience,
        ttlSeconds: config.value.auth.accessTokenTtlSeconds,
        rateLimit: config.value.auth.rateLimit,
        bootstrapAdminEmails: config.value.auth.bootstrapAdminEmails,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Could not start the configured adapters:",
      error instanceof Error ? error.message : error,
    );
    process.exitCode = 1;
    return;
  }

  const { controllers, useCases, close } = container;

  const worker = createOutboxWorker({
    dispatch: useCases.dispatchOutbox,
    intervalMs: config.value.outboxPollIntervalMs,
  });

  const app = buildHttpApp({
    controllers,
    tokens: container.tokens,
    rateLimiter: container.rateLimiter,
    authRateLimiter: container.authRateLimiter,
    metrics: container.metrics,
    healthChecks: container.healthChecks,
  });

  app.log.info(
    {
      persistence: config.value.databaseUrl === null ? "memory" : "postgresql",
      cache: config.value.redisUrl === null ? "memory" : "redis",
      events: config.value.kafkaBrokers === null ? "stdout" : "kafka",
      rateLimit: config.value.rateLimit,
      auth: config.value.auth.usingDevelopmentSecret
        ? "development-secret"
        : "configured-secret",
    },
    "Adapters selected",
  );

  await container.start();
  worker.start();
  registerShutdown(app, worker, close);

  try {
    await app.listen({ port: config.value.port, host: "0.0.0.0" });
  } catch (error) {
    app.log.error(error);
    await worker.stop();
    await app.close();
    await close();
    process.exitCode = 1;
  }
}

await start();

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/architecture/dependency-rules.test.ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, relative } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

/**
 * The lint rules catch imports by path pattern. This test catches them by
 * reading the files, so a new framework import or a `process.env` access in
 * the wrong layer fails even if someone edits the ESLint config.
 */
const SRC = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../src", import.meta.url));

const FRAMEWORKS = [
  "fastify",
  "zod",
  "@prisma/client",
  "ioredis",
  "kafkajs",
  "jose",
  "@aws-sdk/",
  "stripe",
];

function filesUnder(directory: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(directory).flatMap((entry) => {
    const path = join(directory, entry);

    return statSync(path).isDirectory()
      ? filesUnder(path)
      : path.endsWith(".ts")
        ? [path]
        : [];
  });
}

/** Comments describe the rule; only code can break it. */
function codeOnly(contents: string): string {
  return contents.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "").replace(/\/\/.*$/gm, "");
}

function specifiersOf(contents: string): string[] {
  const matches = contents.matchAll(/(?:from\s+|import\s*)["']([^"']+)["']/g);
  return [...matches].map((match) => match[1] ?? "").filter(Boolean);
}

function layer(name: string): { path: string; contents: string }[] {
  return filesUnder(join(SRC, name)).map((path) => ({
    path: relative(SRC, path),
    contents: readFileSync(path, "utf8"),
  }));
}

function forbidden(
  source: { path: string; contents: string }[],
  patterns: string[],
  allowBare = false,
): string[] {
  const violations: string[] = [];

  for (const file of source) {
    for (const specifier of specifiersOf(file.contents)) {
      const bare = !specifier.startsWith(".");

      if (bare && !allowBare) {
        violations.push(`${file.path} imports package "${specifier}"`);
        continue;
      }

      if (patterns.some((pattern) => specifier.includes(pattern))) {
        violations.push(`${file.path} imports "${specifier}"`);
      }
    }
  }

  return violations;
}

describe("dependency rule", () => {
  it("keeps domain free of outer layers, frameworks, and the Node runtime", () => {
    const violations = forbidden(layer("domain"), [
      "/application/",
      "/infrastructure/",
      "/main/",
      ...FRAMEWORKS,
      "node:",
    ]);

    expect(violations).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("keeps application free of infrastructure, frameworks, and the runtime", () => {
    const violations = forbidden(layer("application"), [
      "/infrastructure/",
      "/main/",
      ...FRAMEWORKS,
      "node:",
    ]);

    expect(violations).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("keeps adapters free of the composition root", () => {
    // Adapters legitimately import frameworks and the Node runtime.
    const violations = forbidden(layer("infrastructure"), ["/main/"], true);

    expect(violations).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("keeps ambient time, identity, and environment out of the inner layers", () => {
    const offenders = [...layer("domain"), ...layer("application")].flatMap(
      (file) =>
        ["process.env", "Date.now", "randomUUID"].some((token) =>
          codeOnly(file.contents).includes(token),
        )
          ? [file.path]
          : [],
    );

    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("only lets infrastructure and main be the outermost layers", () => {
    expect(layer("main").length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(forbidden(layer("domain"), ["infrastructure"])).toEqual([]);
  });
});

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/e2e/http/auth-flow.test.ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { assignRole } from "../../../src/domain/auth/account.js";
import {
  createTestHttpApp,
  FIXED_NOW,
  TEST_PASSWORD,
  type TestEnvironment,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

let app: FastifyInstance;
let environment: TestEnvironment;

beforeEach(async () => {
  const created = createTestHttpApp();
  app = created.app;
  environment = created.environment;
  await app.ready();
});

afterEach(async () => {
  await app.close();
});

type AuthBody = {
  customer: { id: string; email: string };
  role: string;
  accessToken: { token: string; tokenType: string; expiresAt: string };
};

async function register(
  email = "ada@example.com",
  name = "Ada",
): Promise<AuthBody> {
  const response = await app.inject({
    method: "POST",
    url: "/auth/register",
    payload: { email, name, password: TEST_PASSWORD },
  });

  expect(response.statusCode).toBe(201);
  return response.json<AuthBody>();
}

async function login(email: string, password = TEST_PASSWORD) {
  return app.inject({
    method: "POST",
    url: "/auth/login",
    payload: { email, password },
  });
}

function bearer(token: string) {
  return { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };
}

async function promoteToAdmin(email: string): Promise<void> {
  const customer = await environment.repositories.customers.findByEmail(email);

  if (customer === null) throw new Error("Expected a registered customer");

  const account = await environment.repositories.accounts.findByCustomerId(
    customer.id,
  );

  if (account === null) throw new Error("Expected an account");

  const promoted = assignRole(account, "admin", FIXED_NOW);
  if (!promoted.ok) throw new Error(promoted.error.message);

  await environment.repositories.accounts.save(promoted.value);
}

async function seedProduct() {
  const result = await environment.useCases.createProduct({
    name: "Mechanical keyboard",
    amountInMinorUnits: 1999,
    currency: "usd",
    availableQuantity: 5,
  });

  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error.message);
  return result.value;
}

describe("authentication over HTTP", () => {
  it("registers, hands back a bearer token, and resolves /auth/me", async () => {
    const session = await register();

    expect(session.customer.email).toBe("ada@example.com");
    expect(session.role).toBe("customer");
    expect(session.accessToken.tokenType).toBe("Bearer");
    // The credential record must never be part of the response.
    expect(JSON.stringify(session)).not.toContain("password");

    const me = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/auth/me",
      headers: bearer(session.accessToken.token),
    });

    expect(me.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(me.json()).toMatchObject({
      customer: { id: session.customer.id },
      role: "customer",
    });
  });

  it("refuses a duplicate registration with 409", async () => {
    await register();

    const duplicate = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/auth/register",
      payload: {
        email: "Ada@example.com",
        name: "Ada again",
        password: TEST_PASSWORD,
      },
    });

    expect(duplicate.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(duplicate.json()).toMatchObject({
      message: expect.stringContaining("already exists"),
    });
  });

  it("logs in and does not reveal which credential was wrong", async () => {
    await register();

    const success = await login("Ada@Example.com");
    expect(success.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(success.json<AuthBody>().accessToken.token.length).toBeGreaterThan(
      0,
    );

    const wrongPassword = await login("ada@example.com", "wrong password");
    const unknownAddress = await login("nobody@example.com");

    expect(wrongPassword.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(unknownAddress.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(wrongPassword.json()).toEqual(unknownAddress.json());
  });

  it("requires a valid bearer token for protected routes", async () => {
    const anonymous = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/auth/me" });

    expect(anonymous.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(anonymous.headers["www-authenticate"]).toContain("Bearer");

    const forged = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/auth/me",
      headers: bearer("forged.token.value"),
    });
    expect(forged.statusCode).toBe(401);

    const customer = await register();
    const cart = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/customers/${customer.customer.id}/cart`,
      headers: bearer(customer.accessToken.token),
    });
    expect(cart.statusCode).toBe(404);
  });

  it("lets only administrators write to the catalogue", async () => {
    const customer = await register();

    const refused = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/products",
      headers: bearer(customer.accessToken.token),
      payload: {
        name: "Mechanical keyboard",
        price: { amountInMinorUnits: 1999, currency: "usd" },
      },
    });

    expect(refused.statusCode).toBe(403);

    await promoteToAdmin("ada@example.com");
    const promoted = await login("ada@example.com");
    const body = promoted.json<AuthBody>();

    expect(body.role).toBe("admin");

    const allowed = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/products",
      headers: bearer(body.accessToken.token),
      payload: {
        name: "Mechanical keyboard",
        price: { amountInMinorUnits: 1999, currency: "usd" },
        availableQuantity: 5,
      },
    });

    expect(allowed.statusCode).toBe(201);
  });

  it("keeps carts, checkout, and orders private", async () => {
    const ada = await register("ada@example.com", "Ada");
    const grace = await register("grace@example.com", "Grace");
    const product = await seedProduct();

    const added = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: `/customers/${ada.customer.id}/cart/items`,
      headers: bearer(ada.accessToken.token),
      payload: { productId: product.id, quantity: 1 },
    });
    expect(added.statusCode).toBe(200);

    const readOtherCart = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/customers/${ada.customer.id}/cart`,
      headers: bearer(grace.accessToken.token),
    });
    expect(readOtherCart.statusCode).toBe(403);

    const checkoutAsOther = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/checkout",
      headers: bearer(grace.accessToken.token),
      payload: { customerId: ada.customer.id },
    });
    expect(checkoutAsOther.statusCode).toBe(403);

    const checkedOut = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/checkout",
      headers: bearer(ada.accessToken.token),
      payload: { customerId: ada.customer.id },
    });
    expect(checkedOut.statusCode).toBe(201);

    const order = checkedOut.json<{ order: { id: string } }>();

    const readOtherOrder = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/orders/${order.order.id}`,
      headers: bearer(grace.accessToken.token),
    });
    expect(readOtherOrder.statusCode).toBe(403);

    const ownOrder = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/orders/${order.order.id}`,
      headers: bearer(ada.accessToken.token),
    });
    expect(ownOrder.statusCode).toBe(200);
  });
});

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/e2e/http/checkout-flow.test.ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  createTestHttpApp,
  TEST_PASSWORD,
  type TestEnvironment,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

let app: FastifyInstance;
let environment: TestEnvironment;

beforeEach(async () => {
  const created = createTestHttpApp();
  app = created.app;
  environment = created.environment;
  await app.ready();
});

afterEach(async () => {
  await app.close();
});

type Session = { id: string; token: string };

async function registerCustomer(email = "Ada@example.com"): Promise<Session> {
  const response = await app.inject({
    method: "POST",
    url: "/auth/register",
    payload: { email, name: "Ada", password: TEST_PASSWORD },
  });

  expect(response.statusCode).toBe(201);

  const body = response.json<{
    customer: { id: string };
    accessToken: { token: string };
  }>();

  return { id: body.customer.id, token: body.accessToken.token };
}

function auth(session: Session) {
  return { authorization: `Bearer ${session.token}` };
}

/**
 * Product creation is an admin action, so the flow seeds the catalogue through
 * the use case instead of the API. The admin route itself is covered by
 * `auth-flow.test.ts`.
 */
async function createProduct(availableQuantity: number) {
  const result = await environment.useCases.createProduct({
    name: "Mechanical keyboard",
    amountInMinorUnits: 1999,
    currency: "usd",
    availableQuantity,
  });

  if (!result.ok) {
    throw new Error(result.error.message);
  }

  return result.value;
}

describe("http checkout flow", () => {
  it("runs customer to paid order through the API", async () => {
    const customer = await registerCustomer();
    const product = await createProduct(5);

    const added = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: `/customers/${customer.id}/cart/items`,
      headers: auth(customer),
      payload: { productId: product.id, quantity: 2 },
    });

    expect(added.statusCode).toBe(200);

    const cart = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/customers/${customer.id}/cart`,
      headers: auth(customer),
    });

    expect(cart.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(cart.json()).toMatchObject({
      itemCount: 2,
      total: { amountInMinorUnits: 3998, currency: "USD" },
    });

    const checkout = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/checkout",
      headers: auth(customer),
      payload: { customerId: customer.id },
    });

    expect(checkout.statusCode).toBe(201);

    const body = checkout.json<{
      order: { id: string; status: string; total: unknown };
      payment: { status: string; providerReference: string };
      replayed: boolean;
    }>();

    expect(body.order.status).toBe("paid");
    expect(body.payment.status).toBe("succeeded");
    expect(body.replayed).toBe(false);

    const order = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/orders/${body.order.id}`,
      headers: auth(customer),
    });

    expect(order.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(order.json()).toMatchObject({ id: body.order.id, status: "paid" });
  });

  it("replays a checkout that carries an Idempotency-Key", async () => {
    const customer = await registerCustomer();
    const product = await createProduct(5);

    await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: `/customers/${customer.id}/cart/items`,
      headers: auth(customer),
      payload: { productId: product.id, quantity: 2 },
    });

    const first = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/checkout",
      headers: { ...auth(customer), "idempotency-key": "order-42" },
      payload: { customerId: customer.id },
    });

    expect(first.statusCode).toBe(201);
    const created = first.json<{
      order: { id: string };
      payment: { providerReference: string };
    }>();

    const second = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/checkout",
      headers: { ...auth(customer), "idempotency-key": "order-42" },
      payload: { customerId: customer.id },
    });

    expect(second.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(second.headers["idempotent-replay"]).toBe("true");
    expect(second.json()).toMatchObject({
      replayed: true,
      order: { id: created.order.id },
      payment: { providerReference: created.payment.providerReference },
    });
  });

  it("rejects a blank Idempotency-Key", async () => {
    const customer = await registerCustomer();

    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/checkout",
      headers: { ...auth(customer), "idempotency-key": "   " },
      payload: { customerId: customer.id },
    });

    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(400);
  });

  it("returns 409 when the cart asks for more than the stock", async () => {
    const customer = await registerCustomer();
    const product = await createProduct(1);

    const added = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: `/customers/${customer.id}/cart/items`,
      headers: auth(customer),
      payload: { productId: product.id, quantity: 2 },
    });

    expect(added.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(added.json()).toMatchObject({
      message: expect.stringContaining("available"),
    });
  });

  it("returns 404 for an unknown order, 400 for malformed input, and 401 without a token", async () => {
    const customer = await registerCustomer();

    const missing = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/orders/nope",
      headers: auth(customer),
    });
    expect(missing.statusCode).toBe(404);

    const malformed = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/products",
      payload: { name: "No price" },
    });
    // The route is admin-only, so the unauthenticated caller is refused first.
    expect(malformed.statusCode).toBe(401);

    const anonymous = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/customers/${customer.id}/cart`,
    });
    expect(anonymous.statusCode).toBe(401);
  });
});
````

````ecommerce-backend/tests/e2e/http/platform-endpoints.test.ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { HealthCheck } from "../../../src/infrastructure/health/health-check.js";
import { buildHttpApp } from "../../../src/infrastructure/http/fastify.js";
import { createMetrics } from "../../../src/infrastructure/observability/metrics.js";
import { createInMemoryRateLimiter } from "../../../src/infrastructure/rate-limit/in-memory-rate-limiter.js";
import {
  createTestAuth,
  createTestEnvironment,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

let app: FastifyInstance | null = null;

afterEach(async () => {
  await app?.close();
  app = null;
});

const healthy: HealthCheck = { name: "postgres", check: async () => undefined };
const broken: HealthCheck = {
  name: "redis",
  check: async () => {
    throw new Error("connection refused");
  },
};

describe("platform endpoints", () => {
  it("reports readiness as ok when every dependency answers", async () => {
    app = buildHttpApp({
      controllers: createTestEnvironment().controllers,
      tokens: createTestAuth().tokens,
      logger: false,
      healthChecks: [healthy],
    });
    await app.ready();

    const response = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/ready" });

    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(response.json()).toEqual({
      status: "ok",
      checks: { postgres: "up" },
    });
  });

  it("returns 503 when a dependency is down", async () => {
    app = buildHttpApp({
      controllers: createTestEnvironment().controllers,
      tokens: createTestAuth().tokens,
      logger: false,
      healthChecks: [healthy, broken],
    });
    await app.ready();

    const response = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/ready" });

    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(503);
    expect(response.json()).toEqual({
      status: "degraded",
      checks: { postgres: "up", redis: "down" },
    });
  });

  it("exposes Prometheus metrics for handled requests", async () => {
    const metrics = createMetrics();

    app = buildHttpApp({
      controllers: createTestEnvironment().controllers,
      tokens: createTestAuth().tokens,
      logger: false,
      metrics,
    });
    await app.ready();

    await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/products" });

    const response = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/metrics" });

    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(response.headers["content-type"]).toContain("text/plain");
    expect(response.body).toContain("http_requests_total");
    expect(response.body).toContain('route="/products"');
    expect(response.body).toContain("checkout_total");
  });

  it("echoes a caller-supplied request id", async () => {
    app = buildHttpApp({
      controllers: createTestEnvironment().controllers,
      tokens: createTestAuth().tokens,
      logger: false,
    });
    await app.ready();

    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/health",
      headers: { "x-request-id": "trace-abc" },
    });

    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(response.headers["x-request-id"]).toBe("trace-abc");
  });

  it("limits callers, exempts health routes, and returns Retry-After", async () => {
    app = buildHttpApp({
      controllers: createTestEnvironment().controllers,
      tokens: createTestAuth().tokens,
      logger: false,
      rateLimiter: createInMemoryRateLimiter({ limit: 2, windowSeconds: 60 }),
    });
    await app.ready();

    await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/health" });
    await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/health" });
    await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/health" });

    const first = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/products" });
    const second = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/products" });
    const third = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/products" });

    expect(first.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(first.headers["x-ratelimit-limit"]).toBe("2");
    expect(second.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(third.statusCode).toBe(429);
    expect(third.headers["retry-after"]).toBeDefined();
    expect(third.json()).toEqual({ message: "Too many requests" });
  });
});
````

````ecommerce-backend/tests/integration/prisma/prisma-persistence.test.ts
import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { dispatchOutboxUseCase } from "../../../src/application/outbox/dispatch-outbox.js";
import type { DomainEvent } from "../../../src/domain/shared/domain-event.js";
import { buildPrismaPersistence } from "../../../src/infrastructure/persistence/prisma/prisma-dependencies.js";
import type { PersistenceDependencies } from "../../../src/infrastructure/persistence/prisma/prisma-dependencies.js";
import {
  buildInMemoryDependencies,
  buildUseCases,
} from "../../../src/main/container.js";
import {
  createTestAuth,
  TEST_PASSWORD,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

const DATABASE_URL = process.env.DATABASE_TEST_URL;

const suite =
  DATABASE_URL === undefined || DATABASE_URL === "" ? describe.skip : describe;

const FIXED_NOW = "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z";

function createEnvironment(persistence: PersistenceDependencies) {
  const base = buildInMemoryDependencies();
  const published: DomainEvent[] = [];
  let sequence = 0;

  const useCases = buildUseCases(
    {
      ...base,
      customers: persistence.customers,
      accounts: persistence.accounts,
      products: persistence.products,
      inventory: persistence.inventory,
      carts: persistence.carts,
      orders: persistence.orders,
      outbox: persistence.outbox,
      unitOfWork: persistence.unitOfWork,
      clock: { now: () => FIXED_NOW },
      ids: { nextId: () => `it-${(sequence += 1)}` },
      events: {
        async publish(event) {
          published.push(event);
        },
      },
    },
    createTestAuth(),
  );

  return { useCases, published };
}

suite("PostgreSQL persistence", () => {
  let persistence: PersistenceDependencies;
  let admin: PrismaClient;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    const url = DATABASE_URL ?? "";

    persistence = await buildPrismaPersistence({ url });
    admin = new PrismaClient({ datasourceUrl: url });
    await admin.$connect();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await persistence.close();
    await admin.$disconnect();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await admin.$executeRawUnsafe(
      'TRUNCATE "Account", "Order", "Cart", "InventoryItem", "Product", "Customer", "OutboxMessage", "ProcessedEvent" CASCADE',
    );
  });

  async function seed(
    useCases: ReturnType<typeof createEnvironment>["useCases"],
  ) {
    const customer = await useCases.register({
      email: "Ada@example.com",
      name: "Ada",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });
    if (!customer.ok) throw new Error("Expected a customer");

    const product = await useCases.createProduct({
      name: "Mechanical keyboard",
      amountInMinorUnits: 1999,
      currency: "usd",
      availableQuantity: 5,
    });
    if (!product.ok) throw new Error("Expected a product");

    return { customer: customer.value.customer, product: product.value };
  }

  it("commits a checkout across every table", async () => {
    const { useCases } = createEnvironment(persistence);
    const { customer, product } = await seed(useCases);

    await useCases.addItem({
      customerId: customer.id,
      productId: product.id,
      quantity: 2,
    });

    const checkout = await useCases.checkout({ customerId: customer.id });
    expect(checkout.ok).toBe(true);

    // Read back through fresh queries, not the in-memory objects.
    const order = await persistence.orders.findByCustomerId(customer.id);
    expect(order).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(order[0]).toMatchObject({ status: "paid" });
    expect(order[0]?.total).toEqual({
      amountInMinorUnits: 3998,
      currency: "USD",
    });

    const stock = await persistence.inventory.findByProductId(product.id);
    expect(stock).toMatchObject({ available: 3, reserved: 0 });

    const pending = await persistence.outbox.findPending(10, FIXED_NOW);
    expect(pending.map((message) => message.event.name)).toEqual([
      "customer.registered",
      "order.created",
      "order.paid",
    ]);

    // The line items survived the JSON round trip as a snapshot.
    expect(order[0]?.items).toEqual([
      {
        productId: product.id,
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: { amountInMinorUnits: 1999, currency: "USD" },
        lineTotal: { amountInMinorUnits: 3998, currency: "USD" },
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("rolls back every write when the business result is a failure", async () => {
    const { useCases } = createEnvironment(persistence);
    const { customer, product } = await seed(useCases);

    await useCases.addItem({
      customerId: customer.id,
      productId: product.id,
      quantity: 1,
    });

    // Empty the shelf after the cart was built, so the order cannot reserve.
    const stock = await persistence.inventory.findByProductId(product.id);
    if (stock === null) throw new Error("Expected stock");
    await persistence.inventory.save({
      ...stock,
      available: 0,
      updatedAt: FIXED_NOW,
    });

    const created = await useCases.createOrder({ customerId: customer.id });
    expect(created.ok).toBe(false);

    expect(await persistence.orders.findByCustomerId(customer.id)).toEqual([]);
    expect(
      (await persistence.outbox.findPending(10, FIXED_NOW)).filter((message) =>
        message.event.name.startsWith("order."),
      ),
    ).toEqual([]);

    const after = await persistence.inventory.findByProductId(product.id);
    expect(after).toMatchObject({ available: 0, reserved: 0 });
  });

  it("persists backoff and dead-letters instead of retrying forever", async () => {
    const { useCases } = createEnvironment(persistence);
    await useCases.register({
      email: "linus@example.com",
      name: "Linus",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });

    let now = FIXED_NOW;
    const clock = { now: () => now };
    const dispatch = dispatchOutboxUseCase({
      outbox: persistence.outbox,
      publisher: {
        async publish() {
          throw new Error("broker unavailable");
        },
      },
      clock,
    });

    await dispatch();

    // The failure is durable and not immediately eligible again.
    expect(await persistence.outbox.findPending(10, now)).toEqual([]);
    const [retry] = await persistence.outbox.findPending(
      10,
      "2026-01-01T00:00:01.000Z",
    );
    expect(retry).toMatchObject({
      attempts: 1,
      lastError: "broker unavailable",
      nextAttemptAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:01.000Z",
    });

    for (let attempt = 2; attempt <= 5; attempt += 1) {
      now = new Date(new Date(now).getTime() + 60_000).toISOString();
      await dispatch();
    }

    const dead = await persistence.outbox.findDeadLettered(10);
    expect(dead).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(dead[0]?.attempts).toBe(5);
  });

  it("claims an event id once, even when the same id arrives twice", async () => {
    const input = {
      id: "evt-1",
      eventName: "order.paid",
      processedAt: FIXED_NOW,
    };

    expect(await persistence.inbox.markProcessed(input)).toBe(true);
    expect(await persistence.inbox.markProcessed(input)).toBe(false);
    expect(await persistence.inbox.hasProcessed("evt-1")).toBe(true);
    expect(await persistence.inbox.hasProcessed("evt-2")).toBe(false);
  });
});

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/integration/redis/redis-adapters.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createRedisCache } from "../../../src/infrastructure/redis/redis-cache.js";
import { createRedisIdempotencyStore } from "../../../src/infrastructure/redis/redis-idempotency-store.js";
import { createRedisRateLimiter } from "../../../src/infrastructure/redis/redis-rate-limiter.js";
import { connectRedis } from "../../../src/infrastructure/redis/redis-client.js";

/**
 * Real Redis, real `SET NX`. Skipped unless a URL is given:
 *
 *   docker compose up -d redis
 *   REDIS_TEST_URL=redis://127.0.0.1:6379 npm test
 */
const URL = process.env.REDIS_TEST_URL;

describe.skipIf(URL === undefined)("redis adapters", () => {
  let connection: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof connectRedis>>;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    connection = await connectRedis(URL as string);
    await connection.client.flushdb();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (connection !== undefined) {
      await connection.close();
    }
  });

  it("caches JSON values with a TTL", async () => {
    const cache = createRedisCache(connection.client);

    await cache.set("product:1", { id: "1", name: "Keyboard" }, 30);

    expect(await cache.get("product:1")).toEqual({
      id: "1",
      name: "Keyboard",
    });

    await cache.delete("product:1");
    expect(await cache.get("product:1")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("claims an idempotency key once and replays a saved outcome", async () => {
    const store = createRedisIdempotencyStore(connection.client);

    expect(await store.acquire("checkout:c1:k1", 30)).toBe("acquired");
    expect(await store.acquire("checkout:c1:k1", 30)).toBe("busy");

    await store.save(
      "checkout:c1:k1",
      { result: { orderId: "order-1" }, createdAt: "now" },
      60,
    );

    expect(await store.find("checkout:c1:k1")).toMatchObject({
      result: { orderId: "order-1" },
    });
    expect(await store.acquire("checkout:c1:k1", 30)).toBe("acquired");
  });

  it("limits a caller across shared connections", async () => {
    const limiter = createRedisRateLimiter(connection.client, {
      limit: 2,
      windowSeconds: 60,
    });

    expect((await limiter.consume("ip:/checkout")).allowed).toBe(true);
    expect((await limiter.consume("ip:/checkout")).allowed).toBe(true);

    const blocked = await limiter.consume("ip:/checkout");
    expect(blocked.allowed).toBe(false);
    expect(blocked.resetInSeconds).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/support/test-environment.ts
import type { Clock } from "../../src/application/ports/clock.js";
import type { EventPublisher } from "../../src/application/ports/event-publisher.js";
import type { IdGenerator } from "../../src/application/ports/id-generator.js";
import type { DomainEvent } from "../../src/domain/shared/domain-event.js";
import { createJwtTokenService } from "../../src/infrastructure/auth/jwt-token-service.js";
import { createScryptPasswordHasher } from "../../src/infrastructure/auth/scrypt-password-hasher.js";
import { buildHttpApp } from "../../src/infrastructure/http/fastify.js";
import { createInMemoryPaymentGateway } from "../../src/infrastructure/payments/in-memory-payment-gateway.js";
import {
  buildControllers,
  buildInMemoryDependencies,
  buildUseCases,
  type AuthDependencies,
  type UseCases,
} from "../../src/main/container.js";

export const FIXED_NOW = "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z";

/** Meets the domain's 12-character floor without being a real credential. */
export const TEST_PASSWORD = "correct horse battery staple";

const TEST_JWT_SECRET = "test-only-secret-that-is-long-enough-32";

type TestEnvironmentOptions = {
  declinePayments?: boolean;
  bootstrapAdminEmails?: readonly string[];
};

export type TestEnvironment = {
  /** The port implementations, including the shared outbox. */
  repositories: ReturnType<typeof buildInMemoryDependencies>;
  /** Events the publisher actually received; empty until the outbox drains. */
  published: DomainEvent[];
  useCases: UseCases;
  controllers: ReturnType<typeof buildControllers>;
  auth: AuthDependencies;
  /** Drains pending outbox messages, as the background worker would. */
  drainOutbox: () => Promise<void>;
};

/**
 * Real auth adapters, with a scrypt cost low enough for a test suite. The
 * verification path, the token format, and the claims are the production ones.
 */
export function createTestAuth(): AuthDependencies {
  return {
    hasher: createScryptPasswordHasher({ cost: { n: 1_024 } }),
    tokens: createJwtTokenService({
      secret: TEST_JWT_SECRET,
      issuer: "ecommerce-backend",
      audience: "ecommerce-api",
      ttlSeconds: 900,
    }),
  };
}

/** Registers through the real use case and returns the customer profile. */
export async function registerTestCustomer(
  environment: TestEnvironment,
  input: { email?: string; name?: string } = {},
) {
  const result = await environment.useCases.register({
    email: input.email ?? "ada@example.com",
    name: input.name ?? "Ada",
    password: TEST_PASSWORD,
  });

  if (!result.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Expected a registration: ${result.error.message}`);
  }

  return result.value.customer;
}

/**
 * Reuses the production wiring and overrides only the ports that tests care
 * about: a fixed clock, predictable ids, captured events, and a gateway that
 * can be told to decline. Same ports, different adapters.
 */
export function createTestEnvironment(
  options: TestEnvironmentOptions = {},
): TestEnvironment {
  const repositories = buildInMemoryDependencies();
  const auth = createTestAuth();

  const clock: Clock = { now: () => FIXED_NOW };
  let sequence = 0;
  const ids: IdGenerator = { nextId: () => `id-${(sequence += 1)}` };

  const published: DomainEvent[] = [];
  const events: EventPublisher = {
    async publish(event) {
      published.push(event);
    },
  };

  const gateway = createInMemoryPaymentGateway(
    options.declinePayments === true ? { declineWhen: () => true } : {},
  );

  const useCases = buildUseCases(
    {
      ...repositories,
      clock,
      ids,
      events,
      gateway,
    },
    auth,
    { bootstrapAdminEmails: options.bootstrapAdminEmails ?? [] },
  );

  return {
    repositories,
    published,
    useCases,
    controllers: buildControllers(useCases),
    auth,
    async drainOutbox() {
      const result = await useCases.dispatchOutbox();

      if (!result.ok) {
        throw new Error(result.error.message);
      }
    },
  };
}

export function createTestHttpApp(options: TestEnvironmentOptions = {}) {
  const environment = createTestEnvironment(options);

  return {
    app: buildHttpApp({
      controllers: environment.controllers,
      logger: false,
      tokens: environment.auth.tokens,
    }),
    environment,
  };
}

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/application/auth.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { assignRole } from "../../../src/domain/auth/account.js";
import {
  createTestEnvironment,
  FIXED_NOW,
  registerTestCustomer,
  TEST_PASSWORD,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

describe("registration", () => {
  it("creates the profile and the credential, then issues a token", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();

    const result = await environment.useCases.register({
      email: "Ada@Example.com",
      name: "Ada",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error.message);

    // The address is normalised, so casing cannot create a second account.
    expect(result.value.customer.email).toBe("ada@example.com");
    expect(result.value.role).toBe("customer");

    const account = await environment.repositories.accounts.findByCustomerId(
      result.value.customer.id,
    );

    expect(account?.role).toBe("customer");
    expect(account?.passwordHash).not.toContain(TEST_PASSWORD);
    expect(account?.passwordHash.startsWith("scrypt$")).toBe(true);

    const principal = await environment.auth.tokens.verify(
      result.value.accessToken.token,
    );

    expect(principal).toEqual({
      customerId: result.value.customer.id,
      email: "ada@example.com",
      role: "customer",
    });
  });

  it("stages customer.registered in the same transaction", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();

    await registerTestCustomer(environment, { email: "linus@example.com" });

    const pending = await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(
      10,
      FIXED_NOW,
    );

    expect(pending.map((message) => message.event.name)).toEqual([
      "customer.registered",
    ]);
  });

  it("refuses a duplicate address and leaves no second record", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    await registerTestCustomer(environment, { email: "ada@example.com" });

    const duplicate = await environment.useCases.register({
      email: "ADA@example.com",
      name: "Ada again",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });

    expect(duplicate.ok).toBe(false);
    if (duplicate.ok) throw new Error("Expected a duplicate rejection");
    expect(duplicate.error.type).toBe("email_already_registered");

    // Neither the customer nor the credential was written.
    expect(
      await environment.repositories.customers.findByEmail("ada@example.com"),
    ).not.toBeNull();
    const all = await Promise.all(
      ["id-1", "id-2", "id-3", "id-4"].map((id) =>
        environment.repositories.accounts.findByCustomerId(id),
      ),
    );
    expect(all.filter((account) => account !== null)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("grants admin only to an address the operator listed for bootstrap", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment({
      bootstrapAdminEmails: ["owner@example.com"],
    });

    const owner = await environment.useCases.register({
      email: "Owner@Example.com",
      name: "Owner",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });
    const shopper = await environment.useCases.register({
      email: "shopper@example.com",
      name: "Shopper",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });

    expect(owner.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(shopper.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!owner.ok || !shopper.ok) throw new Error("Expected registrations");

    expect(owner.value.role).toBe("admin");
    expect(shopper.value.role).toBe("customer");
    expect(
      await environment.auth.tokens.verify(owner.value.accessToken.token),
    ).toMatchObject({ role: "admin" });
  });

  it("refuses a password below the policy before writing anything", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();

    const result = await environment.useCases.register({
      email: "ada@example.com",
      name: "Ada",
      password: "short",
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a rejection");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("invalid_password");

    expect(
      await environment.repositories.customers.findByEmail("ada@example.com"),
    ).toBeNull();
    expect(
      await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(10, FIXED_NOW),
    ).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("login", () => {
  it("returns a session for the right password", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const registered = await registerTestCustomer(environment, {
      email: "grace@example.com",
      name: "Grace",
    });

    const result = await environment.useCases.login({
      email: "Grace@Example.com",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error.message);
    expect(result.value.customer.id).toBe(registered.id);
    expect(result.value.role).toBe("customer");
    expect(
      await environment.auth.tokens.verify(result.value.accessToken.token),
    ).toMatchObject({ customerId: registered.id, role: "customer" });
  });

  it("answers the same way for a wrong password and an unknown address", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    await registerTestCustomer(environment, { email: "ada@example.com" });

    const wrongPassword = await environment.useCases.login({
      email: "ada@example.com",
      password: "not the right password",
    });
    const unknown = await environment.useCases.login({
      email: "nobody@example.com",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });

    expect(wrongPassword.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(unknown.ok).toBe(false);
    if (wrongPassword.ok || unknown.ok) throw new Error("Expected failures");

    expect(wrongPassword.error.type).toBe("invalid_credentials");
    expect(unknown.error.type).toBe("invalid_credentials");
    expect(wrongPassword.error.message).toBe(unknown.error.message);
  });

  it("reports the role that is current in storage, not the one at registration", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const registered = await registerTestCustomer(environment, {
      email: "admin@example.com",
    });

    const account = await environment.repositories.accounts.findByCustomerId(
      registered.id,
    );
    if (account === null) throw new Error("Expected an account");

    const promoted = assignRole(account, "admin", FIXED_NOW);
    if (!promoted.ok) throw new Error("Expected a promotion");
    await environment.repositories.accounts.save(promoted.value);

    const session = await environment.useCases.login({
      email: "admin@example.com",
      password: TEST_PASSWORD,
    });

    expect(session.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!session.ok) throw new Error(session.error.message);
    expect(session.value.role).toBe("admin");
  });

  it("rejects a malformed or unsigned token", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();

    expect(await environment.auth.tokens.verify("not-a-token")).toBeNull();
    expect(await environment.auth.tokens.verify("")).toBeNull();
  });
});
````

````ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/application/checkout-concurrency.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  createTestEnvironment,
  registerTestCustomer,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

async function seed(environment: ReturnType<typeof createTestEnvironment>) {
  const customer = await registerTestCustomer(environment, {
    email: "concurrent@example.com",
    name: "Concurrent",
  });

  const product = await environment.useCases.createProduct({
    name: "One keyboard",
    amountInMinorUnits: 1000,
    currency: "usd",
    availableQuantity: 5,
  });
  if (!product.ok) throw new Error("Expected a product");

  await environment.useCases.addItem({
    customerId: customer.id,
    productId: product.value.id,
    quantity: 1,
  });

  return customer;
}

describe("concurrent checkout", () => {
  it("lets exactly one of two simultaneous requests with the same key proceed", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const customer = await seed(environment);

    const [first, second] = await Promise.all([
      environment.useCases.checkout({
        customerId: customer.id,
        idempotencyKey: "same-request",
      }),
      environment.useCases.checkout({
        customerId: customer.id,
        idempotencyKey: "same-request",
      }),
    ]);

    const results = [first, second];
    const succeeded = results.filter((result) => result.ok);
    const rejected = results.find((result) => !result.ok);

    expect(succeeded).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rejected?.ok).toBe(false);

    if (rejected === undefined || rejected.ok) {
      throw new Error("Expected one rejected request");
    }

    expect(rejected.error.type).toBe("checkout_in_progress");

    // One order, one charge, one set of events.
    const orders = await environment.repositories.orders.findByCustomerId(
      customer.id,
    );
    expect(orders).toHaveLength(1);

    await environment.drainOutbox();
    expect(
      environment.published
        .map((event) => event.name)
        .filter((name) => name.startsWith("order.")),
    ).toEqual(["order.created", "order.paid"]);
  });

  it("releases the claim when an attempt fails, so the key can be reused", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const customer = await registerTestCustomer(environment, {
      email: "retry@example.com",
      name: "Retry",
    });

    // No cart: the attempt fails before any money or stock moves.
    const first = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
      idempotencyKey: "retry-me",
    });
    const second = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
      idempotencyKey: "retry-me",
    });

    expect(first.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(second.ok).toBe(false);

    if (first.ok || second.ok) throw new Error("Expected failures");
    expect(first.error.type).toBe("cart_not_found");
    // Not "checkout_in_progress": the failed attempt released its claim.
    expect(second.error.type).toBe("cart_not_found");
  });
});

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/application/checkout-idempotency.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  createTestEnvironment,
  registerTestCustomer,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

async function seedCheckedOutCart(
  environment: ReturnType<typeof createTestEnvironment>,
) {
  const customer = await registerTestCustomer(environment, {
    email: "grace@example.com",
    name: "Grace",
  });

  const product = await environment.useCases.createProduct({
    name: "Trackball",
    amountInMinorUnits: 4999,
    currency: "usd",
    availableQuantity: 3,
  });
  if (!product.ok) throw new Error("Expected a product");

  await environment.useCases.addItem({
    customerId: customer.id,
    productId: product.value.id,
    quantity: 1,
  });

  return { customer, product: product.value };
}

describe("checkout idempotency", () => {
  it("returns the original order for a repeated key without charging again", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const { customer, product } = await seedCheckedOutCart(environment);

    const first = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
      idempotencyKey: "checkout-attempt-1",
    });

    expect(first.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!first.ok) throw new Error("Expected a checkout");

    const second = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
      idempotencyKey: "checkout-attempt-1",
    });

    expect(second.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!second.ok) throw new Error("Expected a replay");

    expect(second.value.replayed).toBe(true);
    expect(second.value.order.id).toBe(first.value.order.id);
    expect(second.value.payment.providerReference).toBe(
      first.value.payment.providerReference,
    );

    // One order, one charge, one payment's worth of stock committed.
    const orders = await environment.repositories.orders.findByCustomerId(
      customer.id,
    );
    expect(orders).toHaveLength(1);

    const stock = await environment.repositories.inventory.findByProductId(
      product.id,
    );
    expect(stock).toMatchObject({ available: 2, reserved: 0 });

    // The replayed request must not have queued a second pair of events.
    await environment.drainOutbox();
    expect(
      environment.published
        .map((event) => event.name)
        .filter((name) => name.startsWith("order.")),
    ).toEqual(["order.created", "order.paid"]);
  });

  it("keys are scoped per customer, so two customers cannot collide", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();

    const ada = await registerTestCustomer(environment, {
      email: "ada@example.com",
      name: "Ada",
    });
    const grace = await registerTestCustomer(environment, {
      email: "grace@example.com",
      name: "Grace",
    });

    const product = await environment.useCases.createProduct({
      name: "Trackball",
      amountInMinorUnits: 100,
      currency: "usd",
      availableQuantity: 2,
    });
    if (!product.ok) throw new Error("Expected a product");

    for (const customer of [ada, grace]) {
      await environment.useCases.addItem({
        customerId: customer.id,
        productId: product.value.id,
        quantity: 1,
      });
    }

    const adaCheckout = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: ada.id,
      idempotencyKey: "same-key",
    });
    const graceCheckout = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: grace.id,
      idempotencyKey: "same-key",
    });

    expect(adaCheckout.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(graceCheckout.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!adaCheckout.ok || !graceCheckout.ok) throw new Error("Expected both");

    expect(graceCheckout.value.replayed).toBe(false);
    expect(graceCheckout.value.order.id).not.toBe(adaCheckout.value.order.id);
  });

  it("works without a key, as before", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const { customer } = await seedCheckedOutCart(environment);

    const result = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a checkout");
    expect(result.value.replayed).toBe(false);
  });
});

````
````ecommerce-backend/tests/unit/application/checkout.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  createTestEnvironment,
  FIXED_NOW,
  registerTestCustomer,
} from "../../support/test-environment.js";

async function seedCustomerAndProduct(
  environment: ReturnType<typeof createTestEnvironment>,
  availableQuantity: number,
) {
  const customer = await registerTestCustomer(environment);

  const product = await environment.useCases.createProduct({
    name: "Mechanical keyboard",
    amountInMinorUnits: 1999,
    currency: "usd",
    availableQuantity,
  });

  if (!product.ok) throw new Error("Expected a product");

  return { customer, product: product.value };
}

function names(events: { name: string }[]): string[] {
  return events.map((event) => event.name);
}

/** Registration is seeded too; these tests are about the order's events. */
function orderNames(events: { name: string }[]): string[] {
  return names(events).filter((name) => name.startsWith("order."));
}

describe("checkout", () => {
  it("places an order, takes payment, and commits the stock", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const { customer, product } = await seedCustomerAndProduct(environment, 5);

    const added = await environment.useCases.addItem({
      customerId: customer.id,
      productId: product.id,
      quantity: 2,
    });

    expect(added.ok).toBe(true);

    const result = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a completed checkout");

    expect(result.value.order.status).toBe("paid");
    expect(result.value.order.total).toEqual({
      amountInMinorUnits: 3998,
      currency: "USD",
    });
    expect(result.value.payment.status).toBe("succeeded");
    expect(result.value.payment.providerReference).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result.value.replayed).toBe(false);

    const stock = await environment.repositories.inventory.findByProductId(
      product.id,
    );

    expect(stock).toMatchObject({ available: 3, reserved: 0 });

    const cart = await environment.useCases.getCart({
      customerId: customer.id,
    });

    if (!cart.ok) throw new Error("Expected a cart");
    expect(cart.value.itemCount).toBe(0);
  });

  it("queues events in the outbox instead of publishing during the write", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const { customer, product } = await seedCustomerAndProduct(environment, 5);

    await environment.useCases.addItem({
      customerId: customer.id,
      productId: product.id,
      quantity: 1,
    });

    await environment.useCases.checkout({ customerId: customer.id });

    // The transaction committed the events; nothing has been delivered yet.
    expect(environment.published).toEqual([]);

    const pending = await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(
      10,
      FIXED_NOW,
    );
    expect(
      pending
        .map((message) => message.event.name)
        .filter((name) => name.startsWith("order.")),
    ).toEqual(["order.created", "order.paid"]);

    await environment.drainOutbox();

    expect(orderNames(environment.published)).toEqual([
      "order.created",
      "order.paid",
    ]);
    expect(
      await environment.repositories.outbox.findPending(10, FIXED_NOW),
    ).toEqual([]);

    // Draining twice must not redeliver.
    const delivered = environment.published.length;
    await environment.drainOutbox();
    expect(environment.published).toHaveLength(delivered);
  });

  it("cancels the order and releases stock when payment is declined", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment({ declinePayments: true });
    const { customer, product } = await seedCustomerAndProduct(environment, 2);

    await environment.useCases.addItem({
      customerId: customer.id,
      productId: product.id,
      quantity: 2,
    });

    const result = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a decline");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("payment_declined");

    const stock = await environment.repositories.inventory.findByProductId(
      product.id,
    );

    expect(stock).toMatchObject({ available: 2, reserved: 0 });

    const orders = await environment.repositories.orders.findByCustomerId(
      customer.id,
    );

    expect(orders).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(orders[0]?.status).toBe("cancelled");

    await environment.drainOutbox();

    expect(orderNames(environment.published)).toEqual([
      "order.created",
      "order.cancelled",
    ]);
  });

  it("refuses to add more units than are in stock", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const { customer, product } = await seedCustomerAndProduct(environment, 1);

    const added = await environment.useCases.addItem({
      customerId: customer.id,
      productId: product.id,
      quantity: 2,
    });

    expect(added.ok).toBe(false);
    if (added.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(added.error.type).toBe("insufficient_stock");

    // The check must not have moved any stock.
    const stock = await environment.repositories.inventory.findByProductId(
      product.id,
    );
    expect(stock).toMatchObject({ available: 1, reserved: 0 });
  });

  it("refuses to check out an empty cart", async () => {
    const environment = createTestEnvironment();
    const { customer } = await seedCustomerAndProduct(environment, 1);

    const result = await environment.useCases.checkout({
      customerId: customer.id,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) throw new Error("Expected a failure");
    expect(result.error.type).toBe("cart_not_found");
  });
});
````
